# SDS data sources — post-implementation audit (2026-10-09)

Where every field on a generated SDS comes from after the 42 audit items were implemented. The owner-facing summary is the PDF *SDS Data Source Map* (same content, laid out like an SDS). Findings for review are in [sds-data-sources-findings.md](sds-data-sources-findings.md). Nothing was changed by this audit.

Regenerated after the batch E fixes (commit b01bc67): every row describes the current code.

## Summary

Each sheet is now built when it is generated, mostly from data. The formula and each raw material's own data (constituent CAS numbers and percentages, flash point, boiling point, specific gravity, VOC, solubility and physical state) go through the hazard engine. The engine also uses the federal hazard records, your CAS determinations, trade-secret declarations and mixture calculations. The resulting hazard codes then go through the transport classifier and the regulatory lists you maintain: TSCA, SARA 313, HAP, SNUR, Prop 65, RCRA and the carcinogen lists. The final hazard codes choose the translated sentences in Sections 2 and 4 to 14. The supplier block, logo and disclaimer come from Admin Settings, or from the manufacturer record on private-label sheets. Recommended Use comes from the product or its family, and the version and effective date are stamped at publish. A per-product, per-language text edit can replace a single line. The regulated tables and statuses cannot be edited. The same publish gates run on every publish path and in every language.

Before this work, only about a third of a sheet came from data. Most paragraphs were canned: one first-aid, fire, handling and stability text for every product, and Section 14 copied in English from the first ingredient that had a DOT entry. OSHA and TSCA status always gave the same answer, exact percentages were printed, and ES/FR/DE sheets still contained English. Flammability came from the lowest raw-material flash point and from ingredient codes, so the sections could disagree. Now all sections share one Flammable Liquids result, and nearly every paragraph follows the product's own hazards. Fixed text is limited to regulatory wording, format labels and last-resort fallbacks.

**Key points**

- Six things block publishing, on every path and in every language: a blank emergency phone (company, or the manufacturer's on private label), a blank private-label manufacturer name, missing federal hazard data above the threshold (default 1%), a trade-secret ingredient on the Prop 65 list, a Section 14 result of 'Not determined' or an incomplete transport edit, and an inactive finished good. Preview warnings never block.
- Enter a flash point on every liquid raw material. The product flash point is the weight-averaged flash point of the raws that have one, and raws without one are left out. Enter a high flash point for water and other non-flammable liquids, or the average will run low. If no raw has a flash point, the product is treated as not flammable. Boiling points set Category 1 vs 2 and Packing Group I.
- Keep constituent CAS numbers and min/max percentages complete on every raw material. They drive classification, the concentration bands in Sections 3, 8, 11, 12 and 15, the TSCA check and RCRA. Gaps show as warnings, and TSCA then reads 'not verified'.
- Maintain CAS determinations (preferred names, TSCA status, metal and element flags) and the regulatory lists (TSCA, SARA 313 with its PBT and special-concern flags, HAP, SNUR, Prop 65, RCRA waste codes, carcinogens). An edit to them re-queues the affected sheets.
- Maintain each product family's Recommended Use and Restrictions text, plus its active and UV/LED flags, in each language. Also maintain the Admin disclaimer and each manufacturer's disclaimer per language. A blank language prints the translated standard text, never English.
- Text edits are per product and per language and are saved only when they differ from the automatic text. Composition, exposure-limit and regulatory tables, OSHA/TSCA status and the RCRA line cannot be edited. Section 9 temperature edits change only what is displayed.
- Some text stays fixed by regulation: GHS hazard and precautionary wording, the prescribed concentration bands and 0.1% cut-off, the flammability, transport and RCRA thresholds, DOT shipping names, Prop 65 safe-harbor wording and the Appendix D headings.

| Source group | Fields |
|---|---|
| Fields mapped in Sections 1-16 | 281 |
| From your product data | 128 (46%) |
| Standard wording chosen by the product's hazards | 76 (27%) |
| Admin settings and per-product edits | 20 (7%) |
| Fixed text (layout, labels, regulatory phrases) | 57 (20%) |
| Conditions that block publishing | 8 |
| Languages | English, Spanish, French, German (same sources; labels and standard wording translated) |

## Section 1: Identification

Section 1 combines the product's code and description from the CMS import (alias sheets: the alias's base code), use text from a per-product override, the product's own text or its active product family (resale sheets: a standard resale sentence), and the supplier block and logo from Admin > Settings or the private-label manufacturer record. All of it is set in fixed, translated labels and page furniture.

## Section 2: Hazard(s) Identification

Section 2 is built from the hazard classification of the formula. The inputs are ingredient hazard records (PubChem, now attributed per class), competent-person determinations, trade-secret entries, mixture calculations, a Flammable Liquids class derived from the product flash point and boiling point, the optional per-product hazard override and the carcinogen list. All wording comes from fixed GHS phrase and label tables in the sheet language. Two per-product text overrides can change it: Other Hazards and the Section 8 PPE fields. The flammability and physical-state warnings appear only in the preview Warnings box. The missing-data publish block applies on every publish path.

| Field | Source type | Comes from | You maintain | If missing, prints | Technical reference |

## Section 3: Composition / Information on Ingredients

Section 3 is built from the formula. Each raw material's ingredients (CAS number, name and percentage) are combined across the formula and any nested finished goods. The hazard engine, the hazard-data records and the competent-person determinations then decide which ingredients are listed and which H-codes they carry, and the rows print in a fixed table whose headings and notes come from the translation files. Every concentration prints as a prescribed band. A trade-secret identity is replaced by its description. A Substance sheet always names its main constituent. Operator-only warnings appear in the preview Warnings box and are never printed. A published sheet keeps a frozen copy of this section until a new version is published.

## Section 4: First-Aid Measures

Section 4 is built almost entirely from the product's final GHS health hazard codes. Each route paragraph, the symptoms line and the notes to physician are put together from translated sentences chosen by those codes. Three other things can change it. An optional text override, stored per product and per language, replaces a whole field; resale raw materials can now have their own overrides. On UV-family products an uncured-product sentence is added to Skin Contact. The symptoms line is also reused in Section 11.

Sheet language > English > the raw key name. | Nothing | If the sheet language lacks the text, English prints. If the key is missing in every language, the raw key prints in capitals (e.g. "DOCUMENT.SECTION_PREFIX"). ## Section 5: Fire-Fighting Measures

Section 5 is standard translated wording. Each paragraph is chosen from the product's final hazard codes, the same codes Section 2 prints. Flammability now has one source: the Flammable Liquids category (Cat 1-4) comes from the product flash point, which is the wt%-weighted average over the raw materials that have one, together with the initial boiling point and the physical state. A finished-good hazard override can replace that category. Sections 2, 5, 7, 9, 10, 13 and 14 therefore agree. A product whose raw materials carry no flash point is treated as not flammable and gets the default text, unless an FG override sets a category. The flash point sentence prints the same string as Section 9. UV/LED-family products get one extra UV sentence inside Specific Hazards. A per-product override can replace any of the four paragraphs, but the flash point sentence inside a Specific Hazards override is kept current.

## Section 6: Accidental Release Measures

Section 6 is built from fixed, translated sentences. The hazard codes, the product flash point, the physical state and the UV family decide which sentences print. Operators can replace any of the three fields per product (or per resale raw material) and per language.

## Section 7: Handling and Storage

Section 7 is built automatically from the product's final GHS hazard codes and the P-statements Section 2 prints. Fixed base sentences come first, then a sentence for each matching hazard class, then UV sentences for products in an active UV/LED family. Storage ends by repeating the Section 10 incompatible-materials list. Flammable-liquid wording follows the product flash point (the wt%-weighted average over raws that have one), so a product with no flash point data gets no fire wording. A per-language override in the SDS editor (finished goods and resale raw materials) replaces either paragraph entirely, and a Storage override puts a warning on the preview.

| Field | Source type | Comes from | You maintain | If missing, prints | Technical reference |

## Section 8: Exposure Controls / Personal Protection

Section 8 lists the federal and CAS-determination exposure limits for each ingredient, with the Section 3 concentration band and trade-secret identities masked, or prints a fixed 'no exposure limits' sentence. The engineering-control and PPE sentences are chosen automatically from the product's final hazard codes (flammability comes from the product flash point) and its physical state. UV-family products get extra PPE sentences on hazard-driven lines, and each line can be replaced by a per-product override.

Inhalation-only CAS (carbon black, TiO2) and ingredients supplied only by solid/powder raw materials get no rows when any raw material in the product is not solid or powder, and a limit repeated with the same CAS, type, value and units prints once. | Keep the federal data imports current. ## Section 9: Physical and Chemical Properties

Section 9 prints every HazCom Appendix D property, followed by VOC and solids. Eleven lines come from the finished good and its raw materials: the product's own fields, the largest raw material, the lowest value, a weighted average or weighted sums. Twelve Appendix D properties have no data field, so they print "Not determined" unless a value is typed for the product. Seventeen lines can be replaced per product and per language. Labels, the title and the fallback wording come from the translation files.

| Field | Source type | Comes from | You maintain | If missing, prints | Technical reference |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| SECTION 9: PHYSICAL AND CHEMICAL PROPERTIES | translation_or_label_table | Fixed section heading from the language file, printed in capitals. | Nothing | Falls back to the English heading. ## Section 10: Stability and Reactivity

Section 10 is built mostly from the product's final hazard classification (H-codes) and the nitrogen, sulfur and halogen flags on its constituent CAS numbers. Flammability now has one source, the Flammable Liquids category worked out from the product flash point (wt%-weighted over the raws that have one) and the initial boiling point, so Section 10 agrees with Sections 2, 5, 7, 9, 13 and 14. The product family adds the UV item, the translation files supply the wording, and a per-product, per-language override can replace any of the five lines.

| Field | Source type | Comes from | You maintain | If missing, prints | Technical reference |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| SECTION 10: STABILITY AND REACTIVITY | translation_or_label_table | The fixed section heading in the sheet's language, printed in capitals. | Nothing | An old snapshot with no prefix prints the sheet language's prefix (e.g. "SECCIÓN"), or "SECTION" if no translation file defines it. If the title key were missing from every translation file, the key prints in capitals: "SECTION10.TITLE". | meta.document.section_prefix via SDSDocumentStrings::forLanguage (snapshot > document.section_prefix > 'SECTION') + section10.title; mb_strtoupper PDFService:318-325, preview:18/:45 |
| Reactivity / Chemical Stability / Conditions to Avoid / Incompatible Materials / Hazardous Decomposition Products (labels) | translation_or_label_table | Fixed label text in the sheet's language. The decomposition label is wider than the 50 mm PDF label column, so its value prints on the next line. | Nothing | A label key missing from every translation file prints the raw key, e.g. "labels.decomposition_products:"; an old snapshot without the label and no translation prints the field key in title case, e.g. "Decomposition". All five values are always generated and blank override rows are ignored, so no Section 10 line is left out. | meta.labels[key] = t->get('labels.<key>') (getLabels :4767) > SDSDocumentStrings::translate (old snapshots) > ucwords(field key); PDFService FIELD_LABEL_MAP :51-56; preview:620-624 |

Manual hazards on no-CAS raws and trade-secret H-codes (Raw materials screen) count at any level, as does the finished-good hazard override (Finished goods screen). | "No dangerous reaction known under conditions of normal use." | override(10,'reactivity') > section10.reactivity_<class> joined ' ' (H200-205/230-232/240-242/250-252/260-261/270-272) > section10.reactivity; SDSGenerator.php:2799-2851 |

## Section 11: Toxicological Information

Section 11 opens with the likely routes of exposure and the same symptoms line as Section 4. Three acute-toxicity route lines, the chronic-effect fragments and the carcinogen and component block follow. All of it comes from the hazard classification (federal data, CPDs, trade-secret declarations, ATE summation, Finished good hazard override), the carcinogen registry and the exposure-limit datasets. Trade-secret identities are masked. Per product, the Acute Toxicity, Chronic Effects, Carcinogenicity and UV Acrylate Information texts can each be overridden, and the symptoms line follows the Section 4 override.

## Section 12: Ecological Information

Section 12 is built from each constituent's aquatic classifications and M-factors, as summed by the hazard engine or set by a finished-good hazard override, and from the EPA PBT designation on the SARA 313 list. The component table lists only constituents that Section 3 lists, and vendor trade-secret identities are masked. Fixed translation sentences fill anything with no data, and a per-product text override can replace any of the four paragraphs.

## Section 13: Disposal Considerations

Section 13 prints one fixed disposal sentence, which can be replaced for each product (finished goods and resale raw materials). It also prints an RCRA hazardous-waste line that is built when the sheet is generated. That line uses the sheet's final hazard codes, including the Flammable Liquids category derived from the product flash point. It also uses the product's physical state, and a match of each component's CAS number against the admin-maintained EPA RCRA waste-code list, extended to metal compounds through each CAS's RCRA metals flags.

## Section 14: Transport Information

Section 14 is worked out for each product by the transport classifier. Class 3 follows the product's Flammable Liquids category, which comes from the wt%-weighted flash point and the lowest initial boiling point of the raw materials, so Section 14 always agrees with Sections 2, 5, 7, 9, 10 and 13. Classes 8, 6.1 and 9 come from the hazard engine's corrosion, toxicity and aquatic statements, and the UN entry depends on the product's transport type or description and its physical state. Per-product overrides, entered separately for each language (also for resale sheets), win line by line. Every language is checked before publishing. The fixed wording (entry names, notes, bulk and special-precautions lines, labels) comes from the translation files.

| Field | Source type | Comes from | You maintain | If missing, prints | Technical reference |

On old snapshots a line with no value is skipped (the HTML preview still prints the first four labels). | meta.labels > labels.* (en.php:582,697-702,737); PDFService::label() falls back to the key; PDFService.php:1081-1094; preview.php:459-477 |

## Section 15: Regulatory Information

Section 15 is built in SDSGenerator::section15(). It checks the product's composition against the regulatory lists you maintain: the TSCA inventory, SARA 313 (with its metal-compound and glycol-ether categories), HAP, SNUR and Prop 65. It also uses the Section 2 hazard result, one per-product editor note (State Regulations) and fixed translated wording. OSHA and TSCA Status are automatic and cannot be edited per product. Trade-secret ingredient identities are masked on every line.

| Field | Source type | Comes from | You maintain | If missing, prints | Technical reference |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| SECTION 15: REGULATORY INFORMATION (section banner) | translation_or_label_table | Fixed heading from the translation file for the sheet language, printed in capitals. | Nothing | Uses the English wording if the sheet language lacks it. If English is missing too, the key name prints, e.g. 'SECTION 15: SECTION15.TITLE'. | sections[15].title = t->get('section15.title') (SDSGenerator.php:4481); prefix meta.document.section_prefix; mb_strtoupper PDFService.php:318-325, preview.php:45 |
| OSHA Status | hazard_engine_or_cpd | Follows the same classification Section 2 prints: 'classified' when there is a signal word, pictogram, hazard class or H-statement, otherwise 'not classified'. It cannot be edited per product, and stored notes are ignored. | The hazard data behind Section 2: constituent classifications and CPDs on CAS Determinations; flash point and boiling point on raw materials and the product's physical state (Flammable Liquids category); the finished-good hazard override (Finished goods). The sentence itself has no per-product edit. | Nothing classified: 'This product is not classified as hazardous under OSHA HazCom 2024 (29 CFR 1910.1200).' If no raw material has a flash point, the product counts as not flammable. | resolveOshaStatus: isClassified(hazard) ? section15.osha_status : section15.osha_status_not_classified (SDSGenerator.php:4500-4505, :1257-1263); 15.osha_status retired |

Constituents with a CAS on every raw material. | 'TSCA inventory status has not been verified for all components.' prints when a CAS is unchecked, a trade secret has no CAS, or a raw material has no constituents or a blank-CAS constituent. ## Section 16: Other Information

Section 16 is built entirely from publish metadata (the version number and effective date stamped when a sheet is published), the per-language translation files (labels, draft text, abbreviation master table) and the Admin or manufacturer disclaimer text. Nothing in it can be typed per product.

| Field | Source type | Comes from | You maintain | If missing, prints | Technical reference |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| SECTION 16: OTHER INFORMATION (section banner) | translation_or_label_table | Fixed heading from the translation file for the sheet language, printed in capitals on the navy bar. | Nothing | Missing ES/FR/DE text falls back to English. Missing everywhere prints the uppercased key 'SECTION16.TITLE'. Old snapshots without meta.document now get the sheet language's prefix ('ABSCHNITT', 'SECCIÓN'). | sections[16].title = section16.title (section16() SDSGenerator.php:4518); prefix SDSDocumentStrings::forLanguage(meta.document, lang); PDFService::renderSection :315-325; preview.php:6,:18 |
| Version | publish_metadata | The number the system assigns at publish: the next number for that finished good or resale raw material, alias (when a sender publishes it, pack variants share one counter), or private-label item. Unpublished previews print the translated draft text. | Nothing to enter. Never hard-delete published SDS rows (soft-deleted rows still count toward the next number). | Draft or preview: 'Draft (not yet published)' (translated). A pre-version snapshot, now re-rendered only in a private-label record preview, prints no Version line. | section16() :4519 = section16.draft; stampPublishedVersion() :1024-1043 sets it; MAX(sds_versions.version)+1 per base / alias id / alias group (AliasPublisher :216), or private_label_sds |
| Effective Date | publish_metadata | The publish date (today in the admin time zone) written to the version record. It prints as month/day/year in every language. | Nothing | Draft or preview: the line is not printed. A pre-version private-label snapshot in its record preview has no line; the old 'Revision Date' line prints instead. | stampPublishedVersion :1030 format_date(...,'m/d/Y'); publishers pass PublishClock::todayLocal() (alias publishes in SDSController/SDSUpdateController: localDateOfUtc($now)) |
| Abbreviations | translation_or_label_table | A master list of abbreviations for each language. It is filtered to the terms that print elsewhere on that sheet (including the alias or private-label code and the disclaimer) and printed in file order. | Developers keep the four translation tables (en/es/fr/de section16.abbreviation_table) complete and alphabetical. Any new abbreviation in generated text, labels, disclaimers or overrides must be added to all four. | No term matched: the line is not printed. A term missing from the table gets no definition (today SDS/HDS/FDS in the EN/ES/FR Section 4 note, plus ISEA, ASTM, PBT when printed). | AbbreviationService::build :46 (generate :342, generateFromBase :743, alias :835, PL :977); section16.abbreviation_table[lang] > EN; PATTERNS :27; collectCorpus :107-210 |

## Document header, footer, disclaimer, metadata and file names

Document chrome is the page-1 header, section banners, footer, disclaimer, PDF properties, file names and the preview page around the PDF. It comes mainly from company or manufacturer settings, the per-language translation files and the version and date stamped at publish, plus a few fixed layout constants. Alias sheets print the alias base code, and senders always use an alias's own published PDF.

| Field | Source type | Comes from | You maintain | If missing, prints | Technical reference |

It prints on page 1 only. | Upload the company logo (PNG/JPG/GIF, 2 MB max) in Admin > Settings > Company Logo. The file must stay on the server. | No logo prints, but the title and rule still print. Re-renders of older snapshots use today's logo file, or none if the logo was removed or its file type changed. | settings.company.logo_path (AdminController::processLogoUpload) > meta.company_logo_path > PDFService::resolveLogoPath :1384-1391 > SDSTcpdf::Header :97-111 |
| Header logo (page 1) - private label SDS | manufacturer_or_company_settings | The private label manufacturer's own logo. The company logo is never used in its place. | Upload a logo on each private label manufacturer's form. | No logo prints. The company logo is not used instead. | manufacturers.logo_path ('/uploads/manufacturers/mfg-logo-<id>.<ext>') > SDSGenerator::createManufacturerVariant :950 sets meta.company_logo_path |

## Calculated values and their inputs

In this scope, every number, range and derived classification on the sheet is calculated at generation time. The inputs are the formula lines and each raw material's own data: constituent percentages, flash and boiling points, specific gravity, VOC, solubility and physical state. These pass through the hazard engine, the transport classifier and the admin regulatory lists (RCRA, SARA 313/TRI, HAP, carcinogens). Batch E changed several of these rules. The product flash point is now the wt%-weighted average of the raw materials that have one, and a product with no flash point is not flammable. The Flammable Liquids category comes only from that flash point and the initial boiling point. Every Section 9 property in Appendix D prints, with 'Not determined' where there is no data. Trade-secret identities are masked in every section. Missing raw-material data raises warnings in the preview Warnings box, which are never printed. Per-product text overrides still win where they exist. Section 9 flash point and boiling point overrides are printed only when they read as a temperature and agree with the classification.

| Field | Source type | Comes from | You maintain | If missing, prints | Technical reference |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Flammable Liquids class line and H224-H227 (Section 2; drives Sections 5-8, 10, 13, 14) | formula_or_rm_data | A Flammable Liquids class or H224-H227 in the finished-good hazard override > the category from the product flash point and initial boiling point (Cat 1 below 23 °C with IBP 35 °C or less, Cat 2 below 23 °C, Cat 3 up to 60 °C, Cat 4 up to 93 °C). Ingredient flammable classifications are ignored, and Solid, Powder or Paste products are never classified. | Flash Point (°C) and Boiling Point (°C) on raw materials and Physical State (Raw Materials / Finished Goods forms); a Flammable Liquids class in the finished good's hazard override only for a tested product | No flash point on any raw material: not classified as flammable. Flash point below 23 °C with no boiling point: Category 2 is assumed and a preview warning is shown | HazardEngine::applyFlammableLiquidsFromFlashPoint :2849, flammableLiquidCategory :2762; SDSGenerator::flammabilityInputs :4009; FG override :1950-1990 |

Otherwise these CAS are kept out of classification and exposure limits, Prop 65 drops them, and Section 11 keeps the listing with an inhalable-dust note. | Physical State on every raw material (Raw Materials form); the inhalation-only CAS list (Admin > Settings; default carbon black 1333-86-4 and TiO2 13463-67-7) | A raw material with a blank Physical State counts as bound (not dry), so H351 and the Prop 65 listing are suppressed | SDSGenerator::isBoundMixture :4969, inhalationOnlyCasToExclude :4988, applyCarbonBlackLogic :5037, removeInhalationOnlyFromResults :5239; setting sds.inhalation_only_cas |

## Product families and UV products

In this scope, text comes from the product family resolved for each item: its UV/LED flag, its active flag and its per-language Section 1 text. A product gets its family from a manual pick, a rule, or the content vote, where all UV/LED families count together. The UV/LED flag combines with the UV Acrylate Rule Pack switch, which drives the UV sentences folded into Sections 2, 4, 5, 6, 7 and 8 and the Section 11 note. The flag alone drives the Section 7 storage sentence and the Section 10 UV item. Acrylate detection now only supplies the names in the Section 4 sentence. Resale raw-material sheets ignore the family for Section 1. The family name no longer affects anything printed.

| Field | Source type | Comes from | You maintain | If missing, prints | Technical reference |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Recommended Use (Section 1, finished good / intermediate / alias / private-label sheet) | per_fg_override | Per-product, per-language SDS editor text > the product's own Recommended Use field > the family's text in the sheet language > canned translation sentence. Inactive families are ignored, and a blank language never falls back to the family's English text. | Family text in every language on an active family (Settings > Product Families), and the product must resolve to that family. Keep the product's Recommended Use field blank, because it has no language. Put per-language product wording in the SDS editor. | "Printing ink for commercial and industrial applications." (translated on ES/FR/DE). If the family has English text only, ES/FR/DE sheets print their canned translated sentence. | SDSGenerator::resolveUseText (:1134): text_overrides(1,'recommended_use',fg,lang) > finished_goods.recommended_use > family_defaults[lang] (familyFields, active only) > section1.recommended_use |
| Recommended Use (Section 1, resale raw-material sheet) | translation_or_label_table | Per-raw-material, per-language resale SDS editor text > canned resale sentence. The raw material's family is not used. | Nothing normally. For product-specific wording, use the resale SDS editor (/sds/resale/{rm_id}/edit). | "Raw material for industrial formulation." (translated per language) | resolveUseText on resale row (is_resale, recommended_use null; family tier skipped, Q15): text_overrides(raw_material_id,1,'recommended_use') via getOverrides :4628 > section1.recommended_use_resale |
| Restrictions on Use (Section 1, finished good / intermediate / alias / private-label sheet) | per_fg_override | Per-product, per-language SDS editor text > the product's own Restrictions field > the family's restriction text in the sheet language > canned translation sentence. There is no English family fallback, and inactive families are ignored. | Family restriction text in every language (Settings > Product Families). Keep the product's Restrictions field blank: it has no language and masks the family text. | "For professional/industrial use only. Not for household consumer use." (translated per language) | resolveUseText(field='restrictions'): text_overrides(1,'restrictions') > finished_goods.restrictions_on_use > family_defaults['restrictions'][lang] > section1.restrictions |
| Restrictions on Use (Section 1, resale raw-material sheet) | translation_or_label_table | Per-raw-material, per-language resale SDS editor text > canned resale restriction sentence. The family is not used. | Nothing normally; the resale SDS editor is used for exceptions. | "For professional/industrial use only. Not for household consumer use." On UV rule-pack products the field's UV sentence follows the tier sentence (never an override). | Nothing in this scope beyond the family UV/LED flag and the pack switch (the tiers come from H-codes) | Unclassified product: no PPE block in Section 2. Pack off or non-UV family: the tier sentence prints alone. | SDSGenerator::section2 (:1290) → resolvePPE($uvPack) (:2483): text_overrides(8,field) > section8.ppe.<field>.<tier> + section8.uv_<field> |

## Per-product overrides and admin settings

In this scope each printed line is built automatically from formula, hazard-engine and regulatory-list data. If someone has saved a per-product, per-language SDS text edit for that line (text_overrides; on resale sheets the edit is stored on the raw material), the edit prints instead. Some lines take no edit at all: the composition, exposure-limit and regulatory tables, OSHA and TSCA status, the RCRA line, and the fixed Section 14 lines. Admin Settings, product families and manufacturer records supply the supplier block, logo, disclaimer, footnote and Section 1 defaults.

| Field | Source type | Comes from | You maintain | If missing, prints | Technical reference |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Product Identifier | formula_or_rm_data | The finished good's code and description. Alias sheets print the alias's pack-stripped base code, private-label sheets print their own code, and resale sheets print the raw material's base code and supplier product name; no text edit changes this line. | Product code and description on Finished Goods; alias code and description on Item Names; supplier product name on the raw material (resale) | The code alone, e.g. "UVNG009", when the description is blank or repeats the code | productIdentifier() :849-857; createAliasVariant :818 (AliasResolver::stripPack); resale base code :463-469; PL createPrivateLabelVariant :989. Render PDFService.php:378 |
| Recommended Use | per_fg_override | SDS text edit for this language > the finished good's own Recommended Use (one value for all languages) > the active product family's default in the sheet language > standard sentence. Resale sheets skip the family text, and a family with no text in the sheet language falls to the standard sentence, never to its English text. | Per-language defaults on Settings > Product Families (family must be active); leave the finished good's own field blank unless the product really differs | "Printing ink for commercial and industrial applications." (resale: "Raw material for industrial formulation.") | text_overrides[1][recommended_use] > finished_goods.recommended_use > family recommended_use_json[lang] (not resale) > section1.recommended_use(_resale) :1134-1154 |
| Restrictions on Use | per_fg_override | SDS text edit > the finished good's own Restrictions field (one value for all languages) > the active family's default in the sheet language > standard sentence. Resale sheets skip the family text; a blank language never falls to the family's English text. | Per-language family restrictions on Settings > Product Families; keep the finished good's own field blank unless needed | "For professional/industrial use only. Not for household consumer use." | text_overrides[1][restrictions] > finished_goods.restrictions_on_use > family restrictions_json[lang] (not resale) > section1.restrictions(_resale) :1134-1154 |
| Manufacturer / Supplier Information (Company, Address, Phone, Email, Website) | manufacturer_or_company_settings | Admin Settings > Manufacturer Information on standard, resale and alias sheets; the manufacturer record on private-label sheets. The PDF Author property uses the same company name. | Admin > Settings > Manufacturer Information; Manufacturers screen for private-label customers | A blank field drops its line (blank name: PDF Author 'SDS System'); a key never saved prints the config.php placeholder. ## Private label and language behaviour

On a private-label sheet, the base product's content in each language is reused. Only the parts that identify the product and supplier are replaced: the product identity comes from the private-label registry, the supplier block, logo and disclaimer come from the manufacturer record, and the version comes from the private-label item. Every other printed string comes from the per-language translation files. Product data, chemical names and trade-secret descriptions print as entered. A vendor trade-secret identity is masked in every section, in every language.

| Field | Source type | Comes from | You maintain | If missing, prints | Technical reference |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Header logo (page 1, standard sheet) | manufacturer_or_company_settings | The company logo uploaded in Admin Settings. If no logo is uploaded, none prints. | Admin > Settings > Company Logo (upload PNG/JPG/GIF) | No logo is drawn. ## Publish gates and data dependencies

This scope covers the gates, warnings and eligibility rules that decide whether an SDS can be published. Their inputs are admin settings, manufacturer records, raw-material data (flash point, boiling point, constituents, trade-secret flags, review state), CAS determinations, the TSCA inventory, the Prop 65 list and the per-product Section 14 overrides. Since batch E every publish path (manual FG and resale, SDS Update, private label, alias publish for senders, bulk/cron) applies the same blocking gates, in every language. Warnings appear in previews and on the readiness page, and only the TSCA warning is flashed at publish.

| Field | Source type | Comes from | You maintain | If missing, prints | Technical reference |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Gate: company emergency phone blank | admin_setting | Reads only the Emergency Phone saved on Admin Settings. The config-file default is ignored, so the preview and the gate agree; private-label sheets print the manufacturer's number and are not gated on it. | Admin > Settings > Manufacturer Information > Emergency Phone (e.g. 'CHEMTREC: (800) 424-9300'). | Blocks manual FG, resale and alias publish, including an alias publish started by the send queue, auto-send or the shipped-SDS export. SDS Update 'Republish Selected' refuses the whole selection, and the message points to 'Republish Private Labels Only'. Bulk/cron publishes private-label items only (against the latest published base) and marks the job failed with this message, unless no product was due; the Bulk page shows a banner. FG and resale previews list it under Warnings. The readiness page shows 'Company emergency phone missing.' and hides the resale Publish button. | settings company.emergency_phone; SDSReadinessService::companyEmergencyPhoneErrorFromDb 348-352; SDSGenerator::getCompanySettings 4561-4572 |
| Gate: fresh install has no company emergency phone row | admin_setting | The seed script never creates the company emergency phone setting, and the generator skips the config value 'CHEMTREC: (800) 424-9300'. Nothing standard can be published until an admin saves Settings once. | Save Admin > Settings once after install, with Emergency Phone filled in. | Every standard, resale and alias publish is blocked. Bulk publishes private-label items only, against the latest published base version, and the job is marked failed. | seeds/seed.php:279-283 (no company.emergency_phone); config/config.example.php:55 skipped by SDSGenerator.php:4569-4571 |
| Gate: private-label manufacturer emergency phone blank | manufacturer_or_company_settings | A private-label sheet prints its manufacturer's own emergency number, with no fallback to the company number. A blank number refuses that item. | Manufacturers > (manufacturer) > Emergency Phone. The forms refuse a blank value. | The item is refused before a version number is used. On the cascade from FG publish or SDS Update, the base still publishes and an amber 'Private label SDS not regenerated: ...' appears. In bulk the item fails (one work item covering all languages). The private-label preview and the manufacturer page show the error. | manufacturers.emergency_phone via Manufacturer::toCompanyInfo; SDSReadinessService::manufacturerEmergencyPhoneError 359-371; PrivateLabelPublisher::publishOne 350-353 |
| Gate: private-label manufacturer name blank | manufacturer_or_company_settings | A private-label sheet prints the manufacturer's name as the Section 1 Company line, so a blank name refuses the item. The manufacturer forms and the model also refuse to save a blank name. | Manufacturers > Edit > Name. | The item is refused before a version is used. On the cascade an amber 'Private label SDS not regenerated: ...' appears, and in bulk the item fails. | Gate: missing federal hazard data at/above threshold | hazard_engine_or_cpd | Lists every constituent CAS at or above the threshold that has no current federal hazard classification and no active Competent Person Determination. A no-CAS trade-secret bucket whose raws have no manual hazard data saved is listed as TRADE_SECRET; the check runs once per product, on the first language. | For each constituent CAS at or above the threshold, import federal hazard data or create a CAS Determination (CPD). For raws without a CAS, enter the manual hazard data. Keep constituent CAS numbers and percentages accurate on Raw Materials > Constituents. | Blocks every publish path: manual FG and resale, SDS Update, private-label republish and cascade, alias publish for senders, and bulk (FG, resale and private-label items; cached per product). It is not shown in previews or on the readiness page. Below the threshold the sheet publishes silently. Any active CPD clears the gate, even with no hazards ticked; a finished-good hazard override does not. The help text lists manual, resale, SDS Updates, private label and bulk, and says auto-send only emails published SDSs; it does not mention that senders publish missing alias sheets through this gate. | settings sds.block_publish_missing ('0'=off), sds.missing_threshold_pct; settings.php:60-75; SDSReadinessService::missingThresholdInputError/normalise 463-483 |

Keep the Prop 65 list current. | Blocks manual FG and resale publish, SDS Update, private-label republish, alias publish for senders and bulk (FG, resale and private-label items). ## Data you must keep populated (full list)

| Where | Field | Feeds | If blank |
|---|---|---|---|
| Finished goods | Product code and description (CMS import or FG form) | Product Identifier, footer code, PDF title, file names | A blank description prints the code alone ("BK1080") |
| Raw materials | Supplier product name (resale raw materials) | Product Identifier on resale RM SDSs | Prints the RM base code alone |
| Finished goods | Alias customer code and description (CMS import; the Aliases screen is read-only) | Product Identifier and footer code on alias SDSs (code before the first hyphen) | A blank alias description prints the alias base code alone |
| Manufacturers | Private-label item custom code / custom description (optional) | Product Identifier, footer and file name on private-label SDSs | Falls back to the shared alias code/description, then the product code/description |
| Product families | Recommended Use text in each language (EN, ES, FR, DE) | Section 1 Recommended Use (products only, not resale) | A blank language prints that language's standard sentence, never the family's English text |
| Product families | Restrictions on Use text in each language | Section 1 Restrictions on Use (products only, not resale) | A blank language prints that language's standard sentence, never the family's English text |
| Product families | Family rules, active flag, Recompute now, "Reset legacy manual picks to Auto" | Which family text a product gets | With no active family resolved, the standard sentence prints; an inactive family is ignored even when picked manually |
| Finished goods | Product's own Recommended Use / Restrictions (keep blank unless product-specific) | Section 1 use text; outranks the family | Blank is correct and lets family text through. If filled, it prints as typed in every language. |
| SDS editor (per product) | Section 1 Recommended Use / Restrictions override per language (FG editor; resale editor per raw material) | Section 1 use text (highest precedence) | Falls through to the product's own text, then the family text (not resale), then the standard sentence |
| Settings | Company Name | Company line, PDF Author | Line omitted, Author becomes "SDS System", preview warning; never saved = config.php placeholder |
| Settings | Address, City, State, ZIP, Country | Address line | Parts skipped; the line is omitted when all are blank; a never-saved part prints the config.php placeholder |
| Settings | Phone | Phone line | Line omitted with a preview warning; publishing not blocked |
| Settings | Email | Email line | Line omitted |
| Settings | Website | Website line | Line omitted |
| Settings | Emergency Phone | Emergency line on standard, resale and alias SDSs | Line omitted and publishing blocked (the settings form refuses a blank value) |
| Settings | Company logo upload | Page 1 header logo | No logo |
| Settings | Time zone | Effective date in the footer and Section 16 | config app.timezone (America/Chicago by default) |
| Manufacturers | Name | Company line, PDF Author and file name on private-label SDSs | The form refuses it; publishing blocked |
| Manufacturers | Address fields, phone, email, website | Supplier block on private-label SDSs | Each blank line is omitted with no fallback to the base company; a blank phone warns in the preview |
| Manufacturers | Emergency phone | Emergency line on private-label SDSs | The form refuses it; private-label publish refused |
| Manufacturers | Logo | Page 1 header on private-label SDSs | No logo (never the base company's) |
| Raw materials | Current hazard data for each ingredient CAS (PubChem hazard refresh / current source record) | Signal word, pictograms, hazard class lines, Hazard Statements list, H-phrases, P-statements, PPE icons and sentences, Not-classified sentence | The engine logs the CAS as 'no data'. At or above the missing-data threshold, every publish path is blocked unless a determination exists. Below it, the ingredient adds nothing and the sheet can print 'Not a hazardous substance or mixture.' |
| CAS determinations | Competent-person determination (classes, H/P codes, pictograms, signal word) for each CAS without federal data | All Section 2 hazard lines. H-codes and pictograms are limited to the classes that trigger; its P-codes and signal word are used as entered. | Publishing is blocked if the CAS is at or above sds.missing_threshold_pct; otherwise the CAS adds nothing |
| Raw materials | Constituent CAS numbers and percentages | Which hazard records trigger (cut-offs, summation, ATE, carcinogen 0.1% threshold) | The constituent is ignored; hazards can be under-reported |
| Raw materials | Trade-secret manual hazard entry (manual_hazard_json) | Signal word, pictograms, classes, H/P codes. This also applies inside finished-good components, and the ATE and aquatic sums use the raw material's real share. | The trade-secret ingredient contributes no hazards |
| Raw materials | Flash point (°C, '>' flag) and initial boiling point; a high flash point for water and similar raws | Product flash point (wt%-weighted average), and from it the Flammable Liquids class line, H224-H227, GHS02 and its P-codes and signal word | The raw is left out of the average. With no flash point on any raw, no Flammable Liquids class prints. A raw with a flammable ingredient and no flash point raises a preview warning. |
| Raw materials | Physical state on every raw material | Bound-product test for carbon black/TiO2 (any raw not Solid/Powder), and the physical state used for flammability when the finished good has none (highest-wt% raw that has a state) | Counted as bound (not Solid/Powder): carbon black/TiO2 are left out of classification. A product whose state is blank everywhere is treated as a liquid, with a preview warning. |
| Finished goods | Physical state | Flammable Liquids class (Solid, Powder or Paste never gets one) | The physical state of the highest-wt% raw material that has one, else 'Liquid' |
| Regulatory lists | Carcinogen list (agency, classification) per CAS | Carcinogenicity class lines (Category 1A/1B/2), H350/H351, GHS08, signal word, carcinogen P-codes (IARC Group 3 ignored) | No registry line is added; carcinogenicity shows only if the ingredient hazard data has it |
| Finished goods | Hazard override mode and contents (classes, H-codes, P-codes, pictograms, signal word); needs the hazard_override permission | All Section 2 hazard lines; a Flammable Liquids class or H224-H227 replaces the flash-point line | The composition-derived result is used. An override class without its H-code prints the code with no phrase. |
| Product families | UV/LED flag on the family | UV acrylate sentence appended to hazard-driven Section 2 PPE sentences | No UV text |
| Settings | sds.inhalation_only_cas (Admin Settings) | Carbon black/TiO2 exclusion in bound products and the H351 powder rule | Built-in default 1333-86-4 and 13463-67-7 |
| Settings | sds.block_publish_missing and sds.missing_threshold_pct (Admin Settings) | Missing-hazard-data publish block on every publish path | Block on, threshold 1.0% (a blank or out-of-range stored value also gives 1.0%) |
| Settings | uv_acrylate_rule_pack (Admin Settings) | UV PPE sentences | Enabled |
| Settings | Admin > Pictograms artwork uploads (GHS01-09, PPE-eye/hand/respiratory/skin) | GHS and PPE pictogram images | Shipped default images are used |
| SDS editor (per product) | Section 2 Other Hazards override, per language (per raw material on resale sheets) | Other Hazards line | 'None known.' prints |
| SDS editor (per product) | Section 8 PPE overrides (respiratory, hand, eye, skin), per language | Section 2 PPE sentences when that field is hazard-driven | Translated sentence for the H-code level (plus UV text on UV products) |
| Nothing to maintain | Headings, labels, GHS H/P phrase tables, pictogram and PPE captions | Section banner, labels, phrases, captions | n/a |
| Finished goods | Substance / Mixture | Type value; Substance identity row; whether the Mixture notes print | Auto: "Mixture" unless the formula is one raw material marked Substance |
| Raw materials | Substance / Mixture | Type value on single-raw-material finished goods and on resale sheets | "Mixture" |
| Finished goods | Formula line percentages, and a current formula on every finished good used as a component | Concentration bands, the 0.1 % cut-off, row order and which ingredients are listed | A component finished good with no current formula adds no ingredients (readiness-page warning only) |
| Raw materials | Ingredient CAS number | The CAS Number cell, and whether the ingredient can be listed at all | The ingredient is never listed and the preview shows a Composition warning |
| Raw materials | Ingredient chemical name | The Chemical Name cell, when no Prop 65 or CAS master name exists | Blank name |
| Regulatory lists | Prop 65 list chemical name and CAS master preferred name | The Chemical Name cell (they take priority over the typed name) | The typed ingredient name prints |
| Raw materials | Ingredient exact % or min % + max % | Concentration band (summed bounds), the 0.1 % cut-off and row order | Counts as 0 %; the ingredient is dropped and the preview shows a Composition warning |
| Regulatory lists | Federal hazard data and exposure limits per CAS | Which ingredients are listed; the H-Codes cell | The ingredient is omitted unless a CPD exists. Publishing is blocked at or above the missing-data threshold. |
| CAS determinations | Active competent-person determination (classes and exposure limits) | Which ingredients are listed; the H-Codes cell (used only when there is no federal data) | The ingredient is omitted. Publishing is blocked at or above the threshold. |
| Regulatory lists | Carcinogen list | H350/H351 on rows and the listing of carcinogen-only CAS | No carcinogen code; the CAS may not be listed |
| Raw materials | Physical state | Wet-mixture rules: carbon black / TiO2 lose H351 but keep their exposure limits; other powder-only CAS lose their exposure limits | Counts as not solid/powder, so the wet-mixture rules apply |
| Raw materials | Flash point (and initial boiling point) | Whether flammable solvents (at least 1 %) are listed as contributors to the product Flammable Liquids class | Product treated as not flammable; a solvent whose only hazard is flammability is not listed unless it has an exposure limit |
| Raw materials | Ingredient trade-secret checkbox and trade-secret description | TRADE SECRET masking of the CAS and name in Sections 3, 8, 11, 12 and 15 | The name prints "Trade Secret". If no raw material ticks the CAS, its real CAS prints. |
| Raw materials | Hazardous-with-no-CAS flag + manual hazard classification; trade-secret H-codes on CAS-less trade-secret ingredients | The synthetic trade-secret row and its H-Codes cell | No trade-secret row and the hazards are not counted. A CAS-less trade secret without H-codes triggers the Composition warning. |
| Finished goods | Hazard override (add / replace) | H-Codes cells (replace mode blanks every cell; add-mode codes are never credited to a CAS) | The engine result is used |
| Settings | Inhalation-only CAS list (sds.inhalation_only_cas) | Which CAS get the wet-mixture H351 removal and are dropped when nothing is left to report | Built-in defaults 1333-86-4 and 13463-67-7 |
| Settings | Trade-secret description pick-list (sds.trade_secret_descriptions) | Choices for the trade-secret Chemical Name | Free text is still saved, and the list grows automatically when a raw material is saved |
| Settings | Block publish on missing data + missing-data threshold % (0.01-100) | Publish gate for CAS with no hazard data and no CPD | Defaults: blocking on, 1.0 %. A blank, zero or out-of-range stored value uses 1.0 %. |
| Raw materials | Constituent CAS numbers and percentages (exact, or min/max) | Every hazard-driven line (Inhalation, Skin, Eye, Ingestion, Symptoms, Notes to Physician) and the acrylate names in the UV skin sentence | The mixture is under-classified, so the generic base sentences print and the symptoms line can read 'No significant symptoms…' |
| Finished goods | Formula lines and percentages (including sub-product components) | All hazard-driven Section 4 lines | With no composition there are no hazard codes, so only the generic base text prints |
| CAS determinations | Hazard classification sources and competent-person determinations per CAS | All hazard-driven Section 4 lines | That CAS contributes no hazard codes, so its first-aid sentences are missing. At or above the missing-data threshold, publishing is blocked (see gates). |
| Raw materials | Manual hazard JSON (hazardous raws with no CAS) and trade-secret H-codes (also on blank-CAS trade-secret constituents) | All hazard-driven Section 4 lines, on finished-good and resale sheets | Hazards from those raws are lost |
| Raw materials | Physical state on each raw material | Inhalation-only CAS rule: if any raw line is not Solid/Powder, carbon black / TiO2 contribute no hazards at all; if every line is Solid/Powder, each adds H351 at >= 0.1 % | A blank state counts as bound (not powder), so those CAS are left out of classification |
| Finished goods | Hazard override mode (additive / replace) and JSON, where used | All hazard-driven Section 4 lines. In 'replace' mode the carcinogen-registry and powder H351 additions are skipped. Resale raw-material sheets never use it. | The calculated classification is used |
| Regulatory lists | Carcinogen lists (CarcinogenService registry) | Symptoms line, Delayed group (H350/H351) | Carcinogenicity from the registry is not restated in 4(b) |
| Settings | sds.inhalation_only_cas (carbon black and TiO2 are used when it is empty) | Which CAS the inhalation-only rule applies to, which changes every hazard-driven line | The built-in default CAS list is used |
| Settings | uv_acrylate_rule_pack | The UV/EB-curable sentence at the end of Skin Contact | A missing row counts as enabled |
| Product families | is_uv flag, active status, and the family rules and manual picks that set the product's family (UV families are pooled by total share) | The UV/EB-curable sentence at the end of Skin Contact | The sentence is not added |
| Raw materials | Constituent chemical name, trade-secret flag and trade-secret description | Acrylate names inside the UV/EB-curable sentence | A known-CAS acrylate with no name prints its built-in label, and any other acrylate with no name is not detected. A trade secret prints its description or the translated "Trade Secret". |
| SDS editor (per product) | Optional override text for inhalation, skin, eyes, ingestion, symptoms and notes, per language (finished goods at /sds/{id}/edit; resale raw materials at /sds/resale/{rm}/edit) | The matching Section 4 line on this product and on its alias and private-label sheets. The symptoms override also prints in Section 11. | The automatic text prints. Blank rows are ignored and removed on save. Old frozen automatic text is removed by scripts/cleanup-legacy-overrides.php or with 'Reset to automatic'. |
| Nothing to maintain | Translation files en/es/fr/de (section4.*, labels.*, ghs_{lang} h_statements) | All Section 4 wording and labels | Falls back to English, then to the raw key |
| Raw materials | Flash point (°C) and the 'greater than' tick on every raw material that has one (a high value for water and similar raws) | Product flash point (wt%-weighted average). This sets the Flammable Liquids category that chooses the Suitable, Unsuitable, Specific Hazards and Advice text, and it gives the flash point sentence (also Sections 2, 7, 9, 10, 13 and 14) | That raw material is left out of the average. If no raw material has a flash point, the product is not flammable: default Section 5 text, no flash point sentence, and Section 9 prints 'Not determined' |
| Raw materials | Initial boiling point (°C) on solvent raw materials | Cat 1 vs Cat 2 when the product flash point is below 23 °C (lowest raw boiling point is used) | Cat 2 is assumed and the preview shows a warning |
| Finished goods | Physical state | Whether the product is assessed as a liquid. Solid, Powder and Paste are never Flammable Liquids | The physical state of the highest-wt% raw material that has one is used, else 'Liquid' (with a preview warning) |
| Raw materials | Constituent CAS numbers and percentages | The hazard codes behind the water-reactive, oxidizer, organic peroxide and explosive tiers | No hazard codes are carried in, and the generic default text prints |
| CAS determinations | Hazard classifications/H-codes per CAS (H260/H261, H271/H272, H240-H242, H200-H205), with a competent-person determination when no federal or vendor data exist | Suitable and Unsuitable media, Specific Hazards sentences, Advice for Firefighters. Ingredient H224-H227 no longer set the product's flammability | The default text prints. A CAS at or above the missing-data threshold with no data blocks publishing |
| Raw materials | Manual hazard entries on hazardous no-CAS (trade-secret) raw materials | The same non-flammability tiers (counted at 100%, no cut-off). Their Flammable Liquids codes are ignored | Those ingredients add no fire hazard codes |
| Finished goods | Hazard override H-codes/classes and mode (add / replace), for a tested flash point category | All Section 5 tiers. A Flammable Liquids class or H224-H227 here replaces the flash-point-derived category | Calculated codes only |
| SDS editor (per product) | Section 9 Flash Point edit (per language, a temperature with unit) | The flash point sentence and Section 9 (printed only when it agrees with the classification) | The formula-derived flash point is used |
| SDS editor (per product) | Section 5 overrides: Suitable, Unsuitable, Specific Hazards, Firefighter Advice (per language, finished good or resale raw material) | The matching paragraph, which the override replaces in full (the flash point sentence in Specific Hazards stays current) | Automatic text prints |
| SDS editor (per product) | Review of override rows left after the legacy cleanup pass | Any Section 5 paragraph | Frozen old wording that the cleanup did not match keeps printing |
| Settings | UV acrylate rule pack (uv_acrylate_rule_pack) | Specific Hazards UV/EB sentence | A missing setting counts as enabled |
| Product families | UV/LED flag (is_uv) and Active status on the family; family rules | Specific Hazards UV/EB sentence | No UV sentence |
| Finished goods | Product family (automatic from formula content, or a manual pick; the raw material's family on a resale sheet) | Specific Hazards UV/EB sentence | No UV sentence |
| Nothing to maintain | Section heading and field labels | Banner and labels | n/a (translation files) |
| CAS determinations | Current hazard classifications and competent-person determinations for every ingredient CAS: acute toxicity categories and ATE values, skin corrosion, aquatic acute and chronic with M-factors, and pyrophoric, water-reactive, flammable gas or flammable solid codes | Which Personal Precautions paragraph prints, the corrosive add-on, the H220-H223/H228 flammable add-on, the H250/H260/H261 ignition add-on, and every aquatic sentence in Environmental Precautions | Only the standard paragraph and the drains sentence print, so a toxic, corrosive or aquatic-hazard product gets no matching spill advice. A no-data CAS at or above the Missing Data Threshold blocks publishing. |
| Raw materials | Flash Point (°C) and its '>' flag on every raw material, including a high value for water and similar raws | Product flash point (wt%-weighted over raws that have one), which sets Flammable Liquids Cat 1-4. If none has one, the product counts as not flammable and neither add-on prints. |
| Raw materials | Constituent percentages (exact/min/max) and formula line percentages | Whether each hazard class passes its cut-off or ATEmix, and the weights of the flash point average, so every hazard-driven sentence in Section 6 | Classes can drop out and the matching sentences do not print |
| Raw materials | Trade-secret H-codes on withheld constituents; manual hazard data on hazardous no-CAS raw materials | Personal Precautions paragraph choice, corrosive and ignition add-ons, aquatic sentences (trade-secret flammable-liquid codes are not used; the flash point decides) | Hazards of secret ingredients are ignored in Section 6 |
| Finished goods | Hazard override mode / hazard override (optional) | Hazard codes for every Section 6 hazard sentence on finished-good sheets. A Flammable Liquids class or H224-H227 in the override replaces the flash-point category. | Calculated hazard codes are used |
| Finished goods | Physical State | Containment and Cleanup text (solid/powder, gel/paste, gas or liquid). Solid, Powder or Paste also means no Flammable Liquids category. | The state of the largest raw material that has one is used |
| Raw materials | Physical State (at least on the largest raw material in each formula; on resale raw materials for their own sheets) | Containment and Cleanup text and the flammability state when the finished good has no state | Raws without a state are skipped. If no raw has one, 'Liquid' is assumed, the dam-and-absorb text prints and the preview shows a warning. |
| Settings | sds.physical_states (finished-good dropdown list) | Which states can be chosen. Only Solid, Powder, Gel, Paste and Gas change the containment text. | Built-in list Liquid/Paste/Solid/Powder/Gel/Gas |
| Product families | UV/LED flag, Active flag and family-assignment rules (auto family on finished goods and resale raw materials; 'Reset legacy manual picks to Auto') | Containment and Cleanup UV add-on | No family, an inactive family or a non-UV family: no UV sentence |
| Settings | UV acrylate rule pack toggle | Containment and Cleanup UV add-on | Missing setting counts as enabled |
| Settings | Block publishing on missing hazard data, and the Missing Data Threshold (%) | Whether a product whose Section 6 was built without hazard data for an ingredient can be published | Missing block setting counts as on. A blank or invalid threshold falls back to the config default (1%). |
| SDS editor (per product) | Section 6 overrides: Personal Precautions, Environmental Precautions, Containment and Cleanup (per language, on the finished-good or resale raw-material editor) | Replaces the whole field, add-ons included, on this product and on its private-label and alias sheets | Automatic text prints |
| CAS determinations | GHS hazard classifications (H-codes) and P-codes for each CAS, including physical hazards at 1% or more of the formula (Flammable Liquids rows are ignored for the mixture) | Every Handling and Storage hazard sentence except the flammable-liquid trigger, 'Store locked up' (via P405), and the incompatible-materials list | Only the base Handling and Storage sentences and the default incompatibles list print. | Raw materials | Flash point in °C with the '>' flag on every raw, including a high value for water and similar raws | The product flash point (wt%-weighted average over raws that have one) and so H224-H227, which drive the ignition, grounding/static, flammables-area and halogens wording | That raw is left out of the average. If no raw has one, the product is treated as not flammable and gets no fire wording. Raws with a flammable-liquid ingredient and no flash point raise a preview warning. |
| Finished goods | Physical state (else the dominant raw material's state, else 'Liquid') | Solid, Powder and Paste products never get a Flammable Liquids class, so they get no flammable-liquid Section 7 wording | The dominant raw material's state is used, else 'Liquid' with a preview warning |
| Raw materials | Trade-secret hazard codes (manual_hazard_json on hazardous-no-CAS raws, or constituent trade_secret_h_codes) | Handling/Storage hazard sentences and the incompatibles list for products with trade-secret ingredients (H224-H227 excluded) | The trade-secret ingredient's hazards do not trigger any Section 7 sentence |
| Finished goods | Hazard override mode (additive/replace) plus hazard override JSON (e.g. aerosol classes, a Flammable Liquids category, P-codes) | Same as above. | SDS editor (per product) | Section 7 Handling override per language (finished goods: SDS > Edit; resale raw materials: Readiness Check > Edit SDS text) | Replaces the whole Handling paragraph, including the UV sentence | Automatic text prints (base + hazard + UV sentences). | SDS editor (per product) | Section 7 Storage override per language | Replaces the whole Storage paragraph, including the UV, locked-up and Section 10 incompatibles sentences, and shows a preview warning | Automatic text prints, including the Section 10 cross-reference |

| SDS editor (per product) | Republish after any change to hazards, flash points, overrides, family or settings | Every Section 7 line on published PDFs (they print the stored snapshot) | The published sheet keeps the text from publish time |

| Regulatory lists | Federal exposure-limit data (NIOSH, ACGIH TLV and OSHA PEL seed files or the NIOSH connector), kept current | Exposure limits table: all federal rows and the Type, Value, Units and Notes columns | No federal rows. | Raw materials | Constituent exact % or min/max % | Conc% column (summed lower and upper bounds); the 0.01% inclusion cut-off | A constituent with no % counts as 0% and is left out; a max-only % counts as 0 to max |

| Settings | UV acrylate rule pack toggle | UV acrylate PPE sentences | Treated as enabled |

| Finished goods | Hazard override (hazard_override_json / mode) | Reactivity, Chemical Stability, Conditions to Avoid, Incompatible Materials. A Flammable Liquids class or H224-H227 here replaces the flash-point category. | The calculated classification is used (no effect when it is correct) |
| CAS determinations | Nitrogen / sulfur / halogen element flags and molecular formula for each CAS (CAS Descriptions tab, element-flag seeder) | Hazardous Decomposition Products | The base sentence prints without nitrogen oxides, sulfur oxides or hydrogen halides |
| CAS determinations | Re-run the element-flag seeder after new CAS rows are added through CAS Descriptions or TSCA saves, seed data or direct inserts, and check the name-guessed flags on CAS first saved on a raw material | Hazardous Decomposition Products | Those CAS rows keep flags at 0, or the guess from the name, so the base or an incomplete decomposition sentence prints |
| Raw materials | CAS number on each constituent (raw_material_constituents.cas_number) | Hazardous Decomposition Products; hazard lines through CAS data | A constituent with a blank CAS that is not a trade secret with H-codes is dropped from the composition, and its elements and hazards are ignored |
| Product families | UV/LED flag (is_uv) and Active status of each family | Conditions to Avoid: UV light item | The UV item is left off UV products. An inactive family adds nothing. |
| Product families | Family rules or manual family assignment, recompute after imports, and 'Reset legacy manual picks to Auto' | Conditions to Avoid: UV light item | A product in the wrong family, or in none, misses the UV item or gets it when it should not. Legacy manual picks carried over from the old family name stay until they are reset. |
| SDS editor (per product) | Section 10 text overrides per language (reactivity, stability, conditions_avoid, incompatible, decomposition). Finished goods use SDS > Edit; resale sheets use Readiness Check > 'Edit SDS text'. | All five Section 10 values; incompatible also feeds Section 7 Storage | Generated text prints (the desired default). Blank rows are ignored. |
| SDS editor (per product) | Review overrides that the two cleanup passes (cleanup-default-overrides.php, cleanup-legacy-overrides.php) kept as operator text, and use 'Reset to automatic' where they freeze old wording | All five Section 10 values and Section 7 Storage incompatibles | The frozen text keeps printing, e.g. 'Stable' on a reactive product |
| Nothing to maintain | Section banner, field labels, CO/CO2 decomposition base wording | Section 10 heading, labels and decomposition sentence | Not applicable |
| CAS determinations | Acute-toxicity class per route (oral, dermal, inhalation) and LD50/LC50 (ATE) values for each CAS without federal data | Acute toxicity route lines, ATEmix, symptoms line, Ingestion/Inhalation in Likely Routes | Route prints 'Not classified based on available data.' Publishing is blocked when the CAS is at or above the Missing Data Threshold and has no active CPD. |
| CAS determinations | Sensitisation, mutagenicity, carcinogenicity, reproductive, STOT RE and skin/eye classifications | Chronic Effects fragments; 'Delayed:' part of the symptoms line | Fragment omitted. 'None known…' prints when no other fragment applies. |
| CAS determinations | Exposure limits in the CPD (CAS without federal hazard data) | Component exposure limit lines, whether a component row prints, Inhalation in Likely Routes | Only federal limits print. |
| Regulatory lists | Federal hazard data refresh, including ATE columns (ate_oral_mg_kg, ate_dermal_mg_kg, ate_inhalation_vapor_mg_l_4h, ate_inhalation_dust_mg_l_4h) | Acute route lines, ATEmix, chronic fragments, symptoms line | GHS category default ATEs are used, and classification relies on CPDs. |
| Regulatory lists | Carcinogen registry (storage/data/seed/carcinogens.csv, one correct row per CAS+agency; no admin screen) | Chronic carcinogenicity fragment, Carcinogenicity paragraph, component listing lines | The negative 'No components … listed' sentence prints. IARC Group 3 rows are never reported. |
| Regulatory lists | Exposure-limit datasets (OSHA PEL, ACGIH TLV, NIOSH) | Component exposure limit lines, whether a component row prints, Inhalation in Likely Routes | No limit lines. The component row is omitted unless it is carcinogen-listed. |
| Raw materials | Constituent CAS, chemical name and pct_exact / pct_min / pct_max | Every route line, chronic fragments, carcinogen lines, component rows and bands | Ingredient is not evaluated or listed. A row with no % counts as 0. |
| Raw materials | Trade-secret flag and trade-secret description per constituent; trade-secret H-codes or hazard declaration for a no-CAS trade-secret raw material | Masked name in the component block and Carcinogenicity lines; acute/chronic classification; ATEmix (counted at the raw material's real share) | A blank description prints 'Trade Secret'. Without a declaration the trade-secret hazards are not counted. |
| Raw materials | Physical state | Carcinogen and exposure-limit filtering (solid/powder in liquid), inhalable-dust note, all-powder H351 rule | Blank counts as not solid/powder (bound): listed powders in liquids may be dropped, and inhalation-only substances get the dust note. |
| Finished goods | Physical state (falls back to the dominant raw material's state, then Liquid) | Inhalation ATEmix unit (dusts/mists for Solid/Powder, vapours otherwise) | Dominant raw material state is used, otherwise Liquid (vapour unit). |
| Finished goods | Hazard override (additive or replace) | Acute route lines (no basis sentence for override-only categories), chronic fragments, symptoms line | Calculated classification is used. In 'replace' mode the registry and the powder rule add no H350/H351. |
| Product families | Family resolution on the Finished good (manual pick, rules, content share) plus the family's UV flag (is_uv) and active status | UV acrylate note | Note not printed. Inactive families are ignored. |
| Settings | Airborne/Unbound Particles Override (sds.inhalation_only_cas) | Inhalable-dust note, all-powder H351, carcinogen and exposure-limit handling for those CAS | Defaults to carbon black (1333-86-4) and titanium dioxide (13463-67-7). |
| Settings | uv_acrylate_rule_pack | UV acrylate note | A missing row counts as enabled. Any value other than 'enabled' turns the note off. |
| Settings | Missing Data Threshold (sds.missing_threshold_pct) and block switch (sds.block_publish_missing) | Missing-hazard-data publish gate | Blank, 0 or out-of-range values fall back to the config default (1%). With no settings row, the block stays on. |
| SDS editor (per product) | Section 11 overrides: Acute Toxicity, Chronic Effects, Carcinogenicity, UV Acrylate Information (per language; finished goods and resale raw materials) | Replaces the whole paragraph or note | Automatic text prints. |
| SDS editor (per product) | Section 4 'Most Important Symptoms/Effects' override | Symptoms line in both Section 4 and Section 11 | The derived Acute/Delayed text prints. |
| Nothing to maintain | Likely Routes of Exposure | Section 11 routes line, derived from H-codes and exposure limits | Always prints at least 'Skin Contact, Eye Contact.' |
| CAS determinations | Aquatic acute/chronic categories and M-factors, for any CAS that has no current federal hazard data | Ecotoxicity paragraph and H statements; Aquatic Acute/Chronic cells; table rows | The constituent gets no aquatic class, so there is no table row and the mixture may print a no-data or not-classified sentence when it should be classified. A determination is ignored for any CAS that already has current federal data. A missing M-factor prints "(M = 1, default)". |
| Regulatory lists | Federal hazard classifications (aquatic rows, m_factor_acute, m_factor_chronic) under a current hazard source record | Ecotoxicity classification; component table categories and M-factors | No aquatic class. When the class name is unreadable, the category is inferred from the row's H codes. A blank M-factor prints "(M = 1, default)", and the summation then under-weights a potent constituent. |
| Raw materials | Constituent CAS number, percentage (exact or min/max), name | Summation input; component table CAS, Conc% and Chemical Name; Section 3 listing (which decides table rows); PBT matching | No row for a constituent without a CAS; wrong band or classification; blank name cell. |
| Raw materials | Trade-secret flag, trade-secret description, trade-secret H codes; manual hazard JSON on no-CAS hazardous raw materials | Masked table row and PBT item; summation, counted at the raw material's real share of the product | Without the flag, the real name and CAS print. A blank description prints "Trade Secret". Without H codes or JSON, the trade secret adds nothing to the aquatic classification. |
| Finished goods | Formula line percentages | Constituent concentrations, bands and the summation | Wrong classification and wrong bands. |
| Finished goods | Hazard override mode (additive/replace) and hazard override JSON | Aquatic H codes; which lead-in prints (manufacturer's assessment); replace mode drops the component table | The calculated classification and summation lead-in are used. |
| Regulatory lists | SARA 313 list: cas_number, chemical_name, is_pbt | Persistence and Degradability; PBT component item; Bioaccumulative Potential | "No data available." prints even when a PBT chemical is present. |
| Regulatory lists | CAS master molecular formula and names (PubChem import), used to match the seeded TRI lead and mercury compound categories | PBT detection for compounds that are not individual SARA 313 rows | A lead or mercury compound with no formula on file and no keyword in its name is not recognised as PBT. |
| Regulatory lists | Prop 65 list chemical name and CAS master preferred name | Component table Chemical Name | The raw-material constituent name is used instead. |
| SDS editor (per product) | Section 12 text overrides (ecotoxicity, persistence, bioaccumulation, mobility), per language | The matching paragraph (the table still prints). A Persistence override makes Bioaccumulation carry the full PBT sentence. | Automatic text prints; a blank stored row is ignored. Stored copies of earlier automatic text are removed by the cleanup pass. |
| Settings | Missing hazard data block (sds.block_publish_missing) and threshold (sds.missing_threshold_pct) | Whether a product with constituents lacking hazard data, and so possibly lacking aquatic data, can be published | A missing setting row means the block is on. A blank, zero or out-of-range threshold uses the config default (1%). |
| Settings | Show GHS Sections 12–15 note (sds.show_ghs_section_note) | Shared footnote printed after Section 15 (replaces the old Section 12 note) | Missing setting row = shown. |

| Raw materials | Physical State | Fallback physical state for the finished good, and the state on resale sheets | Resale sheet: 'Liquid'. Finished good: the next-largest raw material that has a state, else 'Liquid' |
| CAS determinations | Hazard classifications of constituents (oxidizer, pyrophoric/self-heating, skin and metal corrosion, water-reactive, explosive, self-reactive, flammable gas/aerosol/solid) | D001 non-flash reasons, D002 and D003 items. | Finished goods | Finished-good hazard override (when used) | The H-codes behind D001, D002 and D003. A Flammable Liquids class set here prints the 'classified as a flammable liquid' reason | The engine classification is used |
| Raw materials | Constituent CAS numbers and percentages (exact, or min/max) | D-code component items, trade-secret fragments and the listed-waste reference sentence | A constituent with no CAS or no percentage is never matched, so its RCRA codes do not print (a composition warning shows) |
| Regulatory lists | RCRA Waste Codes (CAS, code, kind D/F/K/P/U, TCLP limit mg/L) on Regulatory Data > RCRA Waste Codes | D-code component items, TCLP limits and the F/K/P/U reference sentence. Element D004-D011 rows also feed metal compounds | No codes print and the sheet says no component has been identified. | CAS determinations | Preferred chemical name (cas_master) / Prop 65 name | Component name in the RCRA fragments (formula products) | The raw-material constituent name, then the CAS number |
| Raw materials | Constituent chemical name, trade-secret flag and trade-secret description | Component name in RCRA fragments (resale sheets use only this name); trade-secret fragments with no code | CAS number; a blank trade-secret description prints 'Trade Secret' |
| SDS editor (per product) | Section 13 Disposal Methods text (optional, per language), for finished goods and resale raw materials | Disposal Methods paragraph | The standard 'Dispose of in accordance with all applicable federal, state, and local regulations…' sentence |
| Raw materials | Flash point (°C) and the 'greater than' flag on every raw that has one, including a high value for water and similar non-flammable raws | Product flash point (wt%-weighted average over raws that have one), and from it the Flammable Liquids category: Class 3, UN entry, Packing Group II/III and the combustible note | The raw is left out of the average.
