| Raw materials | Boiling point (°C) on every volatile raw | Packing Group I (lowest raw boiling point 35 °C or below = Flammable Liquids Cat 1) | A flash point below 23 °C gives Cat 2 / PG II, and the preview shows a boiling-point warning. |
| CAS determinations | Flammable-liquid category on each solvent CAS (H224/H225/H226) | Technical names on n.o.s. entries only (no longer the Class 3 decision) | Technical names fall back to the ingredients of raws with a flash point of 60 °C or below. |
| CAS determinations | Skin-corrosion sub-category (1A/1B/1C), metal corrosion (H290) and acute-toxicity category and route on each CAS | Hazard Class (8, 6.1, precedence over 3), Packing Group, UN Number (combination entries) | No Class 8/6.1 is assigned, or PG II is used for Skin Corr. 1 with no sub-category. |
| CAS determinations | Aquatic toxicity data and M-factors (H400/H410/H411) | Environmental Hazards (marine pollutant), Class 9 (UN3082/UN3077) | Prints "Marine pollutant: No" and no Class 9 is assigned. |
| CAS determinations | Accurate physical-hazard codes (no stray explosive, gas, aerosol, flammable-solid, self-reactive, pyrophoric, self-heating, water-reactive, oxidizer or peroxide codes) | Not-determined safety net: any such code blocks publishing | n/a (a code that is present wrongly makes Section 14 "Not determined" and blocks publishing). |
| CAS determinations | Preferred chemical name | Proper Shipping Name technical names in brackets (n.o.s. entries, finished goods); a Prop 65 list name for the CAS takes precedence | Falls back to the raw-material constituent name. | Raw materials | Constituent chemical name and trade-secret flag | Technical names in brackets (the only name source for resale sheets) | No technical name is printed. Trade-secret constituents are never named. |

| Finished goods | Description (whole-word keywords such as COATING, VARNISH, WASH, CLEANER, REDUCER, THINNER, plurals included) | UN Number and Proper Shipping Name when the transport type is Automatic | Defaults to Printing ink (UN1210). |

Gas makes Section 14 "Not determined" | Uses the physical state of the dominant raw that has one, else 'Liquid' with a preview warning. |
| Finished goods | Hazard override (FG hazard classification) | All Section 14 class triggers, including adding or removing H224-H227 (the preview warns when this disagrees with the flash point) | The engine's own classification is used. |

| Raw materials | Supplier product name (resale raw materials) | Keyword rule for the UN Number and Proper Shipping Name on resale sheets | The product code is used as the description, so it usually defaults to Printing ink (UN1210). |
| Nothing to maintain | Section banner, line labels, entry names, notes, bulk and special-precautions sentences, outcome wording | Fixed text on every Section 14 | n/a |

| CAS determinations | TSCA status override (listed / exempt / not listed) | Section 15 TSCA Status | Inventory lookup, else the 'not verified' sentence and warning |
| CAS determinations | Nitrogen / sulfur / halogen flags | Section 10 Hazardous Decomposition Products | Generic CO/CO2 sentence |
| CAS determinations | RCRA metal flags (As, Ba, Cd, Cr, Pb, Hg, Se, Ag) | Section 13 D004-D011 toxicity-characteristic codes | Only direct rcra_waste_codes CAS matches print |
| CAS determinations | Competent-person determinations (CPDs) | Section 2 classification and every hazard-driven sentence; exempts that CAS from the missing-data block | Federal data; possibly a missing-data publish block |
| Manufacturers | Name, contact details, emergency phone, logo, per-language disclaimer | Private-label Section 1, logo and disclaimer | Blank name or emergency phone blocks publishing; blank phone: warning only; no logo; admin/translation disclaimer |
| Regulatory lists | SARA 313 (PBT and special-concern flags), regulatory categories, HAP, SNUR, Prop 65, carcinogen lists, TSCA inventory, RCRA waste codes | Sections 11, 12 persistence/bioaccumulation, 13 RCRA, 15 | 'None' / negative / 'not verified' sentences |
| SDS editor (per product) | Text edits per language (finished goods: SDS > Edit; resale: Readiness Check > Edit SDS text), incl. Section 9 tested values, State Regulations and full four-field Section 14 overrides on every language | The edited line in Sections 1-15, plus cross-feeds (Section 4 symptoms to Section 11, Section 10 incompatible to Section 7 Storage, persistence to bioaccumulation, Section 8 PPE to Section 2) | Automatic text; State Regulations line omitted. Run both cleanup scripts to clear old frozen edits |
| Product families | UV/LED flag (is_uv) | With the pack switch: Section 2/8 PPE UV sentences, Section 4 Skin Contact, Section 5 Specific Hazards, Section 6 Containment, Section 7 Handling sentences, Section 11 note. Alone: Section 7 Storage UV sentence and Section 10 UV item. Also pools UV families in the content vote. | No UV text anywhere on the sheet, even when the product is full of acrylates |
| Product families | Active flag | Whether the family's rules, content shares, manual picks, Section 1 text and UV flag count at all | Inactive family is ignored everywhere. Deactivating it recomputes immediately and flags its members for republish. |
| Product families | Recommended Use default text, per language (EN/ES/FR/DE) | Section 1 Recommended Use on finished-good, intermediate, alias and private-label sheets (not resale) | That language's canned translated sentence prints; the English family text is never used on ES/FR/DE sheets |
| Product families | Restrictions on Use default text, per language | Section 1 Restrictions on Use (not resale) | That language's canned translated sentence prints |
| Product families | Membership rules (exact code / code prefix / description phrase; applies to products, raw materials or both) | Which family every product and raw material gets, and so all family-driven text | No family: canned Section 1 text and no UV text |
| Product families | Recompute after adding or removing rules | Stored family on every item | Old family keeps printing until the next Recompute, CMS import or item save |
| Product families | Reset legacy manual picks to Auto (button shown while migration 053 picks remain) | Family of products still on the manual link copied from the old family name; lets UV content make them UV | Those products keep the old family, and UV raw materials added later do not change it |
| Finished goods | Family pick: Auto vs manual | Family for the product, and whether raw-material UV content can make it UV | Auto: rule, then the content vote (UV families pooled). A manual pick stays fixed unless its family is deactivated. |
| Finished goods | Recommended Use and Restrictions on Use (keep blank) | Section 1 | Blank is correct: the family text in the sheet language applies. If filled, the text prints as typed on every language. Migration 059 cleared values copied from the retired Default Product Use settings. |
| Finished goods | Substance / Mixture setting | Section 3 Type | Auto: Mixture unless the formula is one Substance raw material |
| Raw materials | Family (rule or manual pick) | Content vote that gives finished goods and intermediates their family; UV text on the raw's own resale sheet | The raw does not count toward any family; its resale sheet gets no UV text (Section 1 is unaffected) |
| Raw materials | Substance / Mixture setting | Section 3 Type on resale sheets and single-raw formulas | Mixture |
| Raw materials | Constituent CAS numbers and chemical names (acrylates) | Names in the Section 4 UV skin sentence (at 0.1 % or more) | The Section 4 sentence prints without names; no other UV text depends on it |
| Raw materials | Trade-secret description on secret acrylate constituents | Section 4 UV sentence names | Prints "Trade Secret" (translated) |
| CAS determinations | Preferred name of acrylate CAS numbers | Section 4 UV sentence names (outranks the constituent name) | Constituent chemical name is used |
| Regulatory lists | Prop 65 list chemical name | Section 4 UV sentence names (first in the name chain) | CAS master / constituent name is used |
| Settings | UV Acrylate Rule Pack switch | Section 2/8 PPE UV sentences, Section 4/5/6/7 Handling sentences, Section 11 note (not Section 7 Storage or Section 10) | Missing row counts as on. Switching it off removes those texts from newly generated sheets only. |
| SDS editor (per product) | Section 1 use text; Section 4 Skin, 5 Specific Hazards, 6 Containment, 7 Handling/Storage, 8 PPE, 10 Conditions to Avoid, 11 UV Acrylate Information (per language) | Those fields on product sheets; Section 1 and the same fields on resale sheets through the resale editor | Automatic text applies. An override replaces the whole field, UV sentence included. |
| Settings | Company Emergency Phone | Section 1 Emergency line on every standard, alias and resale sheet | The settings form refuses a blank value. Never saved: publishing of those sheets is blocked on every path (private-label sheets still publish); previews omit the line and warn |
| Settings | Company Name | Section 1 Company line; PDF Author | Company line omitted, PDF Author 'SDS System', preview warning. Never saved: the config value prints, with a warning |
| Settings | Company address, city, state, zip, country | Section 1 Address line | Blank parts skipped; the line is omitted if all are blank |
| Settings | Company phone, email, website | Section 1 Phone / Email / Website lines | That line omitted; a blank phone adds a preview warning |
| Settings | Company Logo | Page 1 header logo on standard sheets | No logo |
| Settings | Legal disclaimer per language (EN, ES, FR, DE) | Disclaimer text on standard sheets, and on PL sheets whose manufacturer has none for that language | Translated default disclaimer |
| Settings | Show Sections 12–15 footnote toggle; UV rule-pack switch | Footnote after Section 15; UV/EB wording | Missing setting = on |
| Manufacturers | Name | PL Section 1 Company; PDF Author; PDF file name tag; PL download name | Refused by the form; an older blank record blocks private-label publish |
| Manufacturers | Emergency Phone | PL Section 1 Emergency line | Private-label publish refused (manual, cascade, bulk); preview warns |
| Manufacturers | Address, city, state, zip, country, phone, email, website | PL Section 1 supplier lines; PL abbreviations | That line omitted (the company's values are never used); a blank phone adds a preview warning |
| Manufacturers | Logo | PL page 1 header logo | No logo |
| Manufacturers | Disclaimer per language | PL disclaimer text; PL abbreviations | Inherits the admin or translated disclaimer for that language |
| Finished goods | Private-label item custom code / custom description, or shared alias | PL Product Identifier; footer code; PDF Title; file names | Base product code and description. An alias that no longer belongs to the product refuses publish. |
| Finished goods | Product code and description (CMS); alias customer code and description | Product Identifier; footer code; PDF Title; file names | A blank description prints the code alone |
| Finished goods | Recommended use / restrictions columns | Section 1 use text in every language (single language) | Family text for that language, then the translated sentence (columns are no longer pre-filled) |
| Product families | Recommended Use and Restrictions per language (active families) | Section 1 use text (standard and PL; not resale) | Translated standard sentence; English family text is never used on other languages |
| Product families | UV/LED flag | UV/EB wording in S4–S8 and the S11 note | Not printed |
| Finished goods | Physical state and colour (standard vocabulary) | Section 9 State, Color, Appearance (translated on ES/FR/DE) | State 'Liquid' with a preview warning; colour line omitted |
| Raw materials | Flash point, boiling point, specific gravity, VOC, solubility | Section 9 values; flammability in Sections 2, 5, 7, 10, 13, 14 | No flash point = not flammable and 'Not determined'; SG 1.0 / VOC 0 with preview warnings |
| Raw materials | Odor; appearance (resale sheets) (English) | Section 9 Odor / Appearance on every language sheet | Translated 'Not determined' / state word |
| Raw materials | Constituent trade-secret box and description | Masked identity in S3, S4 UV names, S8, S11, S12, S13, S15 (English description in every language) | Translated 'Trade Secret'; a trade secret on the Prop 65 list blocks publishing |
| CAS determinations | Preferred chemical name; TSCA override | Chemical names in S3, S8, S11–S15 on all language sheets; TSCA sentence | Raw-material constituent name |
| SDS editor (per product) | Per-language text edits (incl. State Regulations, Other Hazards, Section 9 Appendix D lines, Section 14) | The edited field on that product's sheets, standard and PL | Automatic text in that language; an English edit does not apply to other languages |
| Regulatory lists | Carcinogen, SARA 313 (PBT / special concern), HAP, SNUR, Prop 65, RCRA, TSCA lists (English names) | Sections 11–15 entries on every language sheet | Translated 'none' sentence or block omitted |
| Nothing to maintain | Version, effective date, labels, banners, page size, Creator, Section 14 bulk / special precautions | Section 16, footer, headings, PDF properties, Section 14 | n/a |
| Settings | Emergency Phone (company) | Section 1 Emergency line on every standard, resale and alias SDS; company phone gate | Standard, resale and alias publish are blocked on every path. SDS Update 'Republish Selected' is refused. Bulk publishes private-label items only and marks the job failed. |
| Settings | Company Name and Phone | Section 1 Company and Phone lines; supplier warnings | Never saved: the config-file value prints. Saved blank: the line is dropped. Warning only. |
| Settings | Missing Hazard Data Gate checkbox and Missing Data Threshold (%) | Missing-federal-hazard-data gate on every publish path | Rows absent: on at 1.0%. A blank or out-of-range threshold cannot be saved; an old invalid value acts as 1.0%. |
| Settings | Cron blackout windows / CMS sync auto bulk publish | When bulk publish runs | No blackout; auto bulk publish on. |
| Manufacturers | Emergency Phone (each private-label manufacturer) | Section 1 Emergency line on that manufacturer's private-label SDSs; manufacturer phone gate | That manufacturer's private-label items are refused (the form will not save it blank). |
| Manufacturers | Name (each private-label manufacturer) | Section 1 Company line, PDF Author and file name of private-label SDSs; manufacturer name gate | That manufacturer's private-label items are refused (the form will not save it blank). |
| Manufacturers | Phone (each private-label manufacturer) | Section 1 Phone line on private-label SDSs | No Phone line prints; private-label preview warning only. |
| Raw materials | Flash Point (and the '>' flag) | wt%-weighted product flash point; Flammable Liquids category in Sections 2, 5, 7, 9, 10, 13, 14 | The raw is left out of the average. With no raw carrying one, the product is not flammable and Section 9 prints 'Not determined'. It never blocks; a warning appears when the raw has a flammable-liquid ingredient. |
| Raw materials | Initial Boiling Point | Flammable Liquids Category 1 vs 2; Section 14 Packing Group I; Section 9 boiling point | With a flash point below 23 °C, Category 2 / PG II is assumed and a preview warning appears. |
| Raw materials | Constituents (CAS and percentages) | Sections 2, 3, 11, 12, 15; missing-hazard-data gate; TSCA warning; composition warning | The material is absent from the composition. The preview and readiness page warn, Section 15 prints TSCA 'not verified' (unless only percentages are missing), and no gate blocks. |
| Raw materials | Trade-secret box on constituents | Identity masking in every section; trade-secret Prop 65 gate | Not applicable. A ticked constituent whose CAS is on the Prop 65 list blocks every publish. |
| Raw materials | Manual hazard data (hazardous, no CAS) | Section 2 classification; TRADE_SECRET entry in the missing-data gate | Blocks as 'TRADE_SECRET (x%)' at or above the threshold when no raw in the trade-secret bucket has manual hazard data saved. |
| Raw materials | H-codes / physical state that imply aerosol, gas, flammable solid, oxidizer, peroxide, etc. | Section 14 unsupported-class check | Not applicable. Such codes (or state Gas) make Section 14 'Not determined' and block until a complete override is entered. |
| Raw materials | Specific Gravity and VOC % (or '<1% VOC') | Section 9 specific gravity and VOC values | Counted as SG 1.0 / VOC 0%; preview warning only. |
| Raw materials | Review (any user save of the RM) | Bulk/cron eligibility; readiness page verdict; resale Publish button | The FG, its aliases and its private-label items are left out of bulk with no reason given. |
| CAS determinations | Competent Person Determination for constituent CAS without federal data | Section 2 classification; missing-hazard-data gate; bulk 'reviewed' rule | Publishing is blocked on every path at or above the threshold. |
| CAS determinations | TSCA Review status (listed/exempt/not listed; NOA note for INACTIVE) | Section 15 TSCA sentence; TSCA warning | Section 15 prints 'not verified', and a warning shows on manual publish, in previews and on the readiness page. |
| Regulatory lists | TSCA inventory import | Section 15 TSCA sentence; TSCA warning | Every product prints 'not verified' and warns. |
| Regulatory lists | Federal hazard data import | Section 2 classification; missing-hazard-data gate | The CAS counts as missing data. The import does not bump timestamps, so bulk will not republish afterwards. |
| Regulatory lists | Prop 65 list | Section 15 Prop 65 block; trade-secret Prop 65 gate | No Prop 65 matches, so the gate never fires. |
| SDS editor (per product) | Section 14 overrides: UN Number, Proper Shipping Name, Hazard Class, Packing Group (+ Environmental hazards), each language | Section 14; transport gate; n.o.s. warning | Fields print the automatic value. An incomplete core set blocks publishing in that language. |
| Finished goods | Current formula | All sections | Publish fails: 'No current formula found'. |
| Finished goods | Status (active flag) | Inactive-product gate on every path; bulk and private-label eligibility | An inactive product cannot be published anywhere. |
| Finished goods | Physical State | Sections 6, 8, 9, 14 (Gas = unsupported transport class) | Falls back to the physical state of the highest-wt% raw material that has one; with none, 'Liquid' is used with a preview warning. |
| Raw materials | Physical State | S9 Physical State and Appearance; S6 containment; S8 dust sentence; S13 D002; S14 solid/liquid entry; flammability (Solid/Powder/Paste never flammable); ATE dust route; carbon black/TiO2 rule | Skipped in favour of the next-largest raw material with a state, then 'Liquid' (preview warning); counts as bound for carbon black/TiO2 |
| Raw materials | Flash Point (°C) and '>' flag | S9 Flash Point (wt%-weighted average); Flammable Liquids category in S2, S5, S7, S8, S10, S13 D001 and S14 Class 3/PG | Left out of the average. With none on any raw: 'Not determined' and not flammable. A raw with a flammable-liquid ingredient and no flash point raises a preview warning |
| Raw materials | Boiling Point (°C) | S9 Initial Boiling Point; Flammable Liquids Cat 1 vs 2; S14 Packing Group I | 'Not determined'. With a flash point below 23 °C, Category 2 / PG II is assumed (preview warning) |
| Raw materials | Specific Gravity | S9 Specific Gravity; VOC (lb/gal) | Counted as 1.000; preview warning names the raw material |
| Raw materials | VOC wt% / 'less than 1%' flag | S9 VOC (lb/gal), VOC (wt%), Solids (wt%) | Counted as 0% VOC; preview warning names the raw material |
| Raw materials | Solids wt%, Exempt VOC wt%, Water wt% | S9 Solids (wt%) | Estimated as 100 − VOC − exempt − water (100% solids when nothing is entered) |
| Raw materials | Solubility | S9 Solubility | Excluded from the calculation; 'Not determined' when no raw has a value. 'Negligible' counts as 3% soluble |
| Raw materials | Odor | S9 Odor (highest-weight raw only) | 'Not determined' |
| Raw materials | Appearance | S9 Appearance on resale sheets only | Lower-case physical state, e.g. 'liquid' |
| Raw materials | Constituents: CAS and % (exact or min/max) | S3 rows and bands; S8, S11, S12, S15 bands; RCRA, SARA, HAP, carcinogen and PBT matches; missing-hazard-data gate | A constituent with no % counts as 0 and is dropped from every section |
| Raw materials | Constituents: trade-secret flag, description and declared H-codes | Identity masking in S3, S8, S11, S12, S13 and S15; trade-secret hazards; Prop 65 trade-secret gate | A blank description prints 'Trade Secret'; an unflagged constituent is named |
| Raw materials | Substance/Mixture | S3 Type for single-ingredient and resale products | 'Mixture' |
| Raw materials | Manual HAP entries (haps_data) | S15 HAP rows and total | Only HAP-list and category matches print |
| Finished goods | Physical State | S9 Physical State; S6; S8; S13 D002; S14; flammability test | Falls back to the largest raw material that has a state, then 'Liquid' (preview warning) |
| Finished goods | Color | S9 Color and Appearance | Color line omitted; Appearance prints the lower-case state |
| Finished goods | Transport Product Type (or a clear description) | S14 UN number and proper shipping name for Class 3 products | Keywords in the description pick the entry; defaults to the printing-ink entry (UN1210) |
| Finished goods | Substance/Mixture | S3 Type; Substance identity row | Auto rule (single-ingredient Substance, else Mixture) |
| Finished goods | Hazard override (Flammable Liquids class or H224-H227) | Replaces the flash-point class in S2, S5, S7, S8, S10, S13 and S14 | Category from the formula flash point; a preview warning shows when the override disagrees with the printed flash point |
| CAS determinations | Corrosive, acute-toxicity and aquatic categories; LD50/LC50; M-factors | S11 routes, symptoms, acute toxicity and ATEmix; S12 aquatic table; S13 D002/D003; S14 Class 8/6.1/9 and marine pollutant | Not classified. Default category ATEs are used silently; a missing M-factor prints '(M = 1, default)' |
| CAS determinations | Competent Person Determination for CAS with no federal hazard data | Missing-hazard-data publish gate | Publishing blocked for a no-data CAS at or above the threshold |
| CAS determinations | TC metals per CAS (cas_master.tc_metals) | S13 D004-D011 lines for metal compounds | Only direct RCRA list matches print |
| Regulatory lists | RCRA waste codes | S13 RCRA component lines | 'No RCRA hazardous waste characteristic…' sentence |
| Regulatory lists | SARA 313 list (de minimis, PBT and special-concern/PFAS flags) and TRI categories | S15 SARA 313 lines and range note; S12 PBT line | 'None' sentence |
| Regulatory lists | HAP list and 112(b) categories | S15 HAP rows and total | Only manual HAPs print |
| Regulatory lists | Carcinogen list | S11 carcinogenicity line and component blocks | 'None listed at or above 0.1%' sentence |
| Regulatory lists | Exempt VOC list | S9 Solids (wt%) | Only the raw's own exempt VOC % is used |
| Settings | sds.inhalation_only_cas | Carbon black/TiO2 rule; S3 row inclusion | Default 1333-86-4 and 13463-67-7 |
| Settings | sds.block_publish_missing / sds.missing_threshold_pct | Missing-hazard-data publish gate | Block on, threshold 1% (config default) |
| Settings | sds.physical_states / sds.color_options | Dropdowns for S9 Physical State and Color | Built-in defaults |
| SDS editor (per product) | Section 9 overrides (appearance, odor, boiling point, flash point, solubility) | S9 lines; the flash point edit also feeds the S5 'Flash point:' sentence. S13 and S14 no longer read it | Calculated value. A flash or boiling point edit that is not a temperature or contradicts the classification is not printed (preview warning) |
| SDS editor (per product) | Section 9 Appendix D overrides (odor threshold, pH, melting point, evaporation rate, flammability, limits, vapor pressure/density, partition coefficient, auto-ignition, decomposition, viscosity) | The matching S9 lines | 'Not determined' ('Not applicable' for flammability (solid, gas) on a Liquid) |
| SDS editor (per product) | Section 4 symptoms override | S4 and S11 Most Important Symptoms/Effects | Derived from the H3xx statements |
| SDS editor (per product) | Section 14 overrides (UN number, shipping name, class, PG, environmental hazards) | S14 lines; a complete UN/PSN/class/PG set clears the transport publish block | Classifier output. A UN number or class override without all four core fields blocks publishing |
| Nothing to maintain | Transport in Bulk, Special Precautions for User, SARA 313 range note | S14 and S15 fixed sentences | Always printed |

## Publish gates (technical detail)

| Gate | Effect | Paths | Clears it |
|---|---|---|---|
| Company emergency phone blank (SDSReadinessService::companyEmergencyPhoneErrorFromDb) | blocks publishing | Manual FG publish (SDSController:533), resale publish (:937), SDS Updates republish (SDSUpdateController:305), bulk publish and cron (BulkPublishController:621, cron/bulk-publish.php:80; only private-label items run), alias publish incl. send queue and auto-send (AliasPublisher:177) | Enter an Emergency Phone in Admin > Settings |
| Private-label manufacturer emergency phone blank (manufacturerEmergencyPhoneError) | blocks publishing | PrivateLabelPublisher::publishOne :350, used by manual private-label publish/republish, SDS Updates "Republish Private Labels Only" and bulk/cron (publishItemFromBase) | Enter the manufacturer's emergency phone on the Manufacturers screen |
| Private-label manufacturer name blank (manufacturerNameError) | blocks publishing | PrivateLabelPublisher::publishOne :355 (every private-label path); also shown in the private-label preview Warnings box | Enter the manufacturer's Name on the Manufacturers screen |
| Form validation: blank Emergency Phone in Admin > Settings; blank Name or Emergency Phone on a manufacturer | blocks publishing | Admin > Settings save refused (AdminController:355-363); ManufacturerController::validateName / validateEmergencyPhone; Manufacturer::update throws | Supply the value |
| Inactive finished good (inactiveFinishedGoodError) | blocks publishing | SDSController::publish :520, SDS Updates (SDSUpdateController:321), AliasPublisher:172; bulk publish skips inactive products; shown in the FG preview Warnings box | Set the product's Status to Active (Finished Goods > Edit) |
| Supplier warnings: company name or phone never saved or blank; private-label manufacturer phone blank | warning only | FG preview (SDSController:85), resale preview (:147), SDS Review page (sds-review/index.php:98), private-label preview (createManufacturerVariant :938-945) | Save the Company Name and Phone in Admin > Settings, or the manufacturer's phone |
| Preview Warnings box: "Publishing blocked: the company emergency phone number is blank..." | warning only | FG preview (SDSController:79-82), resale preview (:143-146); the private-label preview shows the manufacturer's version (createManufacturerVariant :932-935) | Enter the emergency phone |
| Missing federal hazard data: a CAS at or above sds.missing_threshold_pct with no hazard data and no active competent-person determination | blocks publishing | Every publish path: single finished-good publish (SDSController:561), resale raw-material publish (:964), SDS Updates republish (SDSUpdateController:355), private-label publisher (PrivateLabelPublisher:214), alias publisher (AliasPublisher:199) and the Bulk Publish worker (publish-worker.php:161, :227). Auto-send never publishes, so it is not gated. | Create an active determination for each listed CAS, refresh federal data, raise the threshold (0.01-100%), or untick sds.block_publish_missing on Admin Settings |
| Flash point warnings: flash point below 23 °C with no boiling point, override Flammable Liquids category versus the formula flash point, a contradicting Section 9 flash or boiling point edit dropped, or a raw material with a flammable ingredient and no flash point | warning only | Preview Warnings box only (HTML preview and PDF preview pages). Not shown in publish messages. | Enter flash points and boiling points on the raw materials, or enter the tested flash point as a Section 9 Flash Point edit that agrees with the hazard override |
| Physical state default: no physical state on the finished good or on any raw material (the sheet assumes Liquid, which affects the Flammable Liquids class and the carbon black/TiO2 rule) | warning only | Preview Warnings box only (HTML preview and PDF preview pages). Not shown in publish messages. | Set Physical State on the finished good, or on the raw materials |
| Missing hazard data: a CAS at or above sds.missing_threshold_pct (default 1.0 %) has no federal hazard data and no active competent-person determination | blocks publishing | FG publish and resale publish (SDSController.php:561, :964), SDS Update republish (SDSUpdateController.php:355), private-label publish (PrivateLabelPublisher.php:214, first language), alias publish (AliasPublisher.php:199) and the bulk/cron worker (publish-worker.php:161, :227). Auto-send never publishes. | Add federal hazard data or an active CPD for the CAS, lower its share below the threshold, or turn off sds.block_publish_missing / raise the threshold in Admin Settings. Ingredients below the threshold are dropped without a warning. |
| Trade-secret Prop 65 conflict: a constituent ticked trade secret has a CAS on the Prop 65 list | blocks publishing | Preview Warnings box (SDSGenerator:323, :725) and readiness page; FG/resale publish (SDSController.php:547, :949), SDS Update (SDSUpdateController.php:332), alias (AliasPublisher.php:198), private label (PrivateLabelPublisher.php:205), bulk worker (publish-worker.php:160, :210) | Have the vendor disclose the chemical and clear its trade-secret box on the raw material, or correct the CAS number. |
| Substance sheet with no CAS constituent (Section 3 cannot name the substance) | warning only | Preview Warnings box / publish flash (SDSGenerator:303-306, :705-708) | Add the CAS constituent on the raw material, or set the product to Mixture. |
| Same CAS ticked trade secret on one raw material and disclosed on another | warning only | Preview Warnings box / publish flash (SDSGenerator::tradeSecretDisclosureWarnings, :320-322) | Make the trade-secret checkbox agree on every raw material that supplies the CAS. |
| Formula raw material with incomplete constituent data (no constituents at all, blank CAS, CAS-less trade secret with no H-codes, or no %) | warning only | Preview Warnings box / publish flash (SDSGenerator:287-290); readiness page | Enter every constituent with its CAS number and percentage on the raw material. |
| Finished-good component with no current formula | warning only | Readiness page (SDSReadinessService::fgComponentsWithoutFormulaWarning, sds-review/index.php:91) | Enter a current formula for the component finished good. |
| Missing federal hazard data: a composition CAS at or above sds.missing_threshold_pct (default 1 %) has no hazard data and no active competent-person determination. This is a sheet-wide gate on the hazard inputs that drive every Section 4 line. | blocks publishing | Finished-good and resale publish, SDS Updates republish, private-label and alias publish, and the bulk/cron publish worker. Auto-send never publishes. | Add hazard data for the CAS, or an active Competent Person Determination on the CAS determinations screen. Otherwise raise the threshold or turn off 'block publish on missing data' in Settings. |
| Missing federal hazard data for a CAS at or above the missing-data threshold with no active competent-person determination | blocks publishing | Manual finished-good and resale publish, SDS Updates republish, private-label publish, alias publish, bulk publish and the cron worker | Add hazard data or a competent-person determination for the CAS, or change sds.missing_threshold_pct or turn off sds.block_publish_missing in Admin Settings |
| Section 9 Flash Point edit contradicts the classification, so it is not printed | warning only | SDS preview Warnings box and publish message | Correct the raw-material flash points, reset the edit, or use a finished-good hazard override for a tested mixture |
| Section 9 Flash Point or Boiling Point edit is not a temperature and is ignored | warning only | SDS preview Warnings box and publish message | Re-enter it with a unit (e.g. '24 °C') or reset it to automatic in the SDS editor |
| Product flash point below 23 °C with no raw-material boiling point (Category 2 assumed) | warning only | SDS preview Warnings box and publish message | Enter boiling points on the solvent raw materials |
| FG hazard override sets a Flammable Liquids category that the printed formula flash point does not support | warning only | SDS preview Warnings box and publish message | Enter the tested flash point as a Section 9 Flash Point edit, or correct the override |
| Raw materials that contain a disclosed flammable-liquid ingredient (1% or more of the product) but have no flash point | warning only | SDS preview Warnings box and publish message | Enter the flash point on those raw materials |
| Missing federal hazard data (no hazard data and no active competent-person determination for a CAS at or above the Missing Data Threshold) | blocks publishing | Manual finished-good and resale publish, SDS Updates republish, bulk and cron publish worker, private-label publish, alias publish | Add hazard classifications or an active competent-person determination for each listed CAS, or turn off the block or raise the threshold in Admin Settings |
| Physical-state default warning (no state on the finished good or any raw material, so 'Liquid' is assumed) | warning only | Preview Warnings box (finished good, resale and computeBase generation); never printed | Set Physical State on the finished good or on a raw material in its formula |
| Missing federal hazard data: a CAS at or above the threshold (default 1%) has no hazard data and no active Competent Person Determination | blocks publishing | Manual finished-good and resale publish, SDS Updates republish (including private label), and bulk/cron publish | Create a Competent Person Determination for the CAS, or switch off the block / raise the threshold in Admin Settings |
| Section 14 'Not determined' (unsupported class): explosive, aerosol, gas, flammable solid, self-reactive, pyrophoric, self-heating, water-reactive or oxidizer H-codes, i.e. the codes behind most Section 7 physical-hazard sentences | blocks publishing | Every publish path, checked for every generated language; the reason is also shown in every preview's Warnings box | Correct the raw-material hazard data, or enter a complete Section 14 override (UN number, proper shipping name, class, packing group) in every language |
| Section 7 warning: Storage is a per-product override and no longer names the Section 10 incompatibles | warning only | Preview Warnings box (finished good, resale, private label); never printed | List the incompatibles in the override, or clear it (Reset to automatic) |
| Flash point warning: raw materials containing a flammable-liquid ingredient have no flash point, so the product may be under-classified in Sections 2, 5, 7, 9, 10, 13 and 14 | warning only | Preview Warnings box and publish flash; never printed | Enter the flash point on the named raw materials |

| Section 14 transport 'Not determined' for unsupported classes. | Missing federal hazard data | blocks publishing | Manual finished-good and resale publish, SDS Updates republish, private-label publish, bulk publish and the cron worker (auto-send never publishes) | Give every constituent CAS at or above the threshold (default 1%) current federal hazard data or an active CAS determination, or switch the block off in Admin Settings. |
| Missing federal hazard data (CAS with no hazard data and no active Competent Person Determination at or above the threshold) | blocks publishing | Manual finished-good and resale publish, SDS Updates republish, private-label publish, alias publish, bulk publish and the cron worker | Create a Competent Person Determination for each listed CAS, or turn off the block / change the threshold (default 1 %) in Admin Settings |
